# Rune Carving — recipe candidates

**41 cards; reviewed authored-operational PLAYTEST with AEX-01–07 fulfilled.** Sources and original book prose remain preserved.

## Refined Vital Binding Rune — recipe-396

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

A prepared plate has 1 lifetime charge(s). First legal creature entry spends one: DC 9 STR save; failure is Restrained for at most 60 seconds. Repeat the save at the end of each regular turn. An Action permits STR (Athletics) or DEX (Acrobatics) escape, or an adjacent helper's STR (Athletics), at that same DC through Core task binary resolution. Moving the target completely outside the original trigger square/volume, or moving, covering, suppressing or Breaking the original plate, ends this particular Binding restraint. Restoring the plate does not resume that ended restraint, refund its charge or rearm its ground trigger. Other independent restraints remain separately governed. Use RC-01–04 entry, placement, next-regular-start/six-second arming delay, cap, discharge and manual rearming rules. No Stun, occupancy retrigger, ally exemption, recharge or repair refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: RC-plate: One steel inscription plate, captive feet and strap; standalone item even when attached to another body. 2 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 4 × 4 × 0.5 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 6 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Rune Carving level 4; INT with actual Runecarver's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 9. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 4 hours; operational 240 active minutes, or 180 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 225/165 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Runecarver's Slab, with Runecarver's tools, Carving stylus, Resonance lens; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=expedition. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["trainer", "starter manual", "common experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": false, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 9, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 2; Standard 4; Exceptional yield 5; Masterwork yield 6. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 4 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Resonance Powder (ing-396-0-resonance_powder): quantity 4; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.05 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.
- [1] warrior's heart (drop-warrior_s_heart): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 148 gp / 0.44 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

On Masterwork retain at most one actual catalyst unit total, from occurrence(s) 0, 1. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

RC-plate: finished unit 259/4 gp, functional repair material 1/2 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 259/16 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 9. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Unchanged source effect**

Triggered rune with 1 charge; a creature must pass a DC 9 Strength save or become restrained for 1 minute.

## Field Mighty Warding Rune — recipe-398

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

**Effect and operation**

This plate has 1 lifetime charge(s) and a +1 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +1 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +1 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

## Field Grave Healing Rune — recipe-399

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Register at most six actually known permitted allied identities. This plate has 1 lifetime charge(s); one eligible entry or Action touch spends one to restore 1d8+2 ordinary HP to one actual eligible living ally. A helper can spend an Action with direct plate contact on an assigned willing or Incapacitated ally within 5 feet under RC-06. Stationary setup/arming and helper healing are separate Actions; moving the plate does not heal creatures along its route. Ordinary healing blockers, eligibility and caps remain: no Dead target resurrection or crafted-Construct reconstruction. A legal full-HP/prevented heal still spends its charge. RC-01–03 and RC-06 enforce one discharge per physical item per round, identity and no refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: RC-plate: One steel inscription plate, captive feet and strap; standalone item even when attached to another body. 2 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 4 × 4 × 0.5 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 6 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Rune Carving level 2; INT with actual Runecarver's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 11. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 hours; operational 120 active minutes, or 90 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 105/75 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

Actual Runecarver's Slab, with Runecarver's tools, Carving stylus, Resonance lens; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=field. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

## Superior Gale Warding Rune of Eagle Bone — recipe-402

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This plate has 1 lifetime charge(s) and a +1 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +1 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +1 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: RC-plate: One steel inscription plate, captive feet and strap; standalone item even when attached to another body. 2 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 4 × 4 × 0.5 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 4 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Rune Carving level 5; INT with actual Runecarver's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 10. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 hours; operational 120 active minutes, or 90 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 105/75 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Runecarver's Slab, with Runecarver's tools, Carving stylus, Resonance lens; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=field. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["guild trainer", "faction vendor", "uncommon dungeon book", "reverse engineering"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 10, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 3; Exceptional yield 3; Masterwork yield 4. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 3 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Carving tools (ing-402-0-carving_tools): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 75 gp / 1.0 lb; role tool; consumed=false.
- [1] Stabilizing matrix (ing-402-1-stabilizing_matrix): quantity 1; tier 3; source unit 25 gp / 0.1 lb; role stabilizer; consumed=true.
- [2] Activation catalyst (ing-402-2-activation_catalyst): quantity 2; tier 3; source unit 25 gp / 0.1 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.
- [3] Eagle bone shard (drop-eagle_bone_shard): quantity 1; tier 2; source unit 38 gp / 0.59 lb; role material; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

On Masterwork retain at most one actual catalyst unit total, from occurrence(s) 2. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

RC-plate: finished unit 164/3 gp, functional repair material 1/2 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 41/3 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 10. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

## Refined Frost Binding Rune — recipe-405

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

A prepared plate has 1 lifetime charge(s). First legal creature entry spends one: DC 8 STR save; failure is Restrained for at most 60 seconds. Repeat the save at the end of each regular turn. An Action permits STR (Athletics) or DEX (Acrobatics) escape, or an adjacent helper's STR (Athletics), at that same DC through Core task binary resolution. Moving the target completely outside the original trigger square/volume, or moving, covering, suppressing or Breaking the original plate, ends this particular Binding restraint. Restoring the plate does not resume that ended restraint, refund its charge or rearm its ground trigger. Other independent restraints remain separately governed. Use RC-01–04 entry, placement, next-regular-start/six-second arming delay, cap, discharge and manual rearming rules. No Stun, occupancy retrigger, ally exemption, recharge or repair refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: RC-plate: One steel inscription plate, captive feet and strap; standalone item even when attached to another body. 2 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 4 × 4 × 0.5 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 3 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Rune Carving level 4; INT with actual Runecarver's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 8. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 hours; operational 120 active minutes, or 90 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 105/75 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Runecarver's Slab, with Runecarver's tools, Carving stylus, Resonance lens; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=field. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

## Refined Ember Rune — recipe-408

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This plate has 1 lifetime charge(s) and a +1 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +1 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +1 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: RC-plate: One steel inscription plate, captive feet and strap; standalone item even when attached to another body. 2 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 4 × 4 × 0.5 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 7 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 hours; operational 120 active minutes, or 90 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 105/75 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

Actual Runecarver's Slab, with Runecarver's tools, Carving stylus, Resonance lens; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=field. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Effect and operation**

This plate has 2 lifetime charge(s) and a +1 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +1 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +1 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Effect and operation**

This plate has 3 lifetime charge(s) and a +2 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +2 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +2 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: RC-plate: One steel inscription plate, captive feet and strap; standalone item even when attached to another body. 2 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 4 × 4 × 0.5 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Rune Carving level 15; INT with actual Runecarver's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 18. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 10 days; operational 4800 active minutes, or 3600 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 4785/3585 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Runecarver's Slab, with Runecarver's tools, Carving stylus, Focusing crystal; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=haven. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 1 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["forbidden archive", "named master crafter", "Gate-zone research", "complex reverse engineering"], "minimumResearchHours": 28, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 20, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Carving tools (ing-419-0-carving_tools): quantity 1; tier 8; source unit 9000 gp / 1.0 lb; role tool; consumed=false.
- [1] Focusing Crystal (ing-419-1-focusing_crystal): quantity 2; tier 8; source unit 3000 gp / 0.1 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [2] Binding agent (ing-419-2-binding_agent): quantity 4; tier 8; source unit 3000 gp / 0.1 lb; role binder; consumed=true.
- [3] blue dragon heartstone (drop-blue_dragon_heartstone): quantity 1; tier 5; source unit 1378 gp / 0.19 lb; role catalyst; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

On Masterwork retain at most one actual catalyst unit total, from occurrence(s) 3. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

RC-plate: finished unit 26762 gp, functional repair material 1/2 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 13381/2 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 18. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

Warding rune with 3 charges; expend one to gain +2 AC and saving throws until the start of your next turn.

**Effect and operation**

Register at most six actually known permitted allied identities. This plate has 3 lifetime charge(s); one eligible entry or Action touch spends one to restore 4d8+6 ordinary HP to one actual eligible living ally. A helper can spend an Action with direct plate contact on an assigned willing or Incapacitated ally within 5 feet under RC-06. Stationary setup/arming and helper healing are separate Actions; moving the plate does not heal creatures along its route. Ordinary healing blockers, eligibility and caps remain: no Dead target resurrection or crafted-Construct reconstruction. A legal full-HP/prevented heal still spends its charge. RC-01–03 and RC-06 enforce one discharge per physical item per round, identity and no refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

## Ascendant Ember Warding Rune of Hellfire Essence — recipe-428

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

**Effect and operation**

This plate has 4 lifetime charge(s) and a +2 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +2 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +2 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

This plate has 5 lifetime charge(s) and a +3 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +3 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +3 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

## Core-Crystal Warding Rune — recipe-664

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This plate has 5 lifetime charge(s) and a +3 ward. Ten active minutes attunes its one bearer; one ward per bearer, one bearer per ward, and death ends attunement. Wear it in one actual wrist, neck or chest jewelry position, taking an Action to don/doff. Before an attack roll targeting the bearer or the bearer's save roll, spend one Reaction and charge for +3 AC in equipment.defense-enhancement and +3 saves in equipment.jewel-save-enhancement through the next regular-turn start, including the triggering roll. Outside initiative use the RC-08 six-second marker. No post-roll activation, second Reaction, free jewelry slot or extra-turn expiry/reset. Removal, meld, suppression or lost eligibility suspends the remaining benefit while its deadline runs. Use RC-07/08's complete timing and comparisons; charges never refill.

**Rune control and examination**

A ground plate that is moved, rotated off its declared working face, wholly covered, Broken, incomplete or suppressed cannot trigger. Restore an invalidated ground trigger only through the actual one-Action manual arming procedure on its cleared eligible square, followed by the existing next-regular-start or six-second delay; merely uncovering, repairing or unsuppressing does not rearm it. A capable authorized setter can disarm their accessible plate with an Action; physically removing it uses its actual item interaction afterward. These ground-trigger rules do not replace the specific direct-touch Healing procedure or the separate attuned-Ward duration procedure.

With safe access within five feet, one active minute permits an INT (Arcana) or INT check using Runecarver's tools against the original recipe creation DC, using normal Core task shifts collapsed to success/failure. Apply only the actual applicable proficiency contribution. This examination reveals supported operating type/trigger, not donor lore, and failure grants no repeat on unchanged evidence. Disarming an unknown accessible Rune requires actual Runecarver's-tools proficiency and tools, one active minute, and that source DC. Failure leaves it armed; it discharges only if an actual eligible entry/touch event occurs, not merely because the check failed. An unrelated tool proficiency is not a substitute.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: RC-plate: One steel inscription plate, captive feet and strap; standalone item even when attached to another body. 2 gp, 1 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 19, dimensions 4 × 4 × 0.5 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Rune Carving level 16; INT with actual Runecarver's tools training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 24. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 1 month; operational 9600 active minutes, or 7200 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 9585/7185 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Runecarver's Slab, with Runecarver's tools, Carving stylus, Focusing crystal; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=haven. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 2 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["forbidden archive", "named master crafter", "Gate-zone research", "complex reverse engineering"], "minimumResearchHours": 32, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 20, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. **Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] core crystal (drop-core_crystal): quantity 1; tier 5; source unit 1421 gp / 0.11 lb; role material; consumed=true.
- [1] Prepared stone (supply-prepared_stone-t9): quantity 1; tier 9; source unit 7500 gp / 1.0 lb; role substrate; consumed=true.
- [2] Runic ink (supply-runic_ink-t9): quantity 1; tier 9; source unit 7500 gp / 0.05 lb; role ingredient; consumed=true.
- [3] Binding agent (supply-binding_agent-t9): quantity 1; tier 9; source unit 7500 gp / 0.1 lb; role binder; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

RC-plate: finished unit 37164 gp, functional repair material 1/2 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 9291 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 24. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

## Aberrant Null-Geometry Seal — recipe-667

**Reviewed internal playtest; director-only; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

This is the custom stationary null seal. Use the full RC-10 procedure below; it has one NEW lifetime activation, not an invented source charges field.

**Complete custom procedure — RC-10**

Preserve source **one hour,20ft,DC28 CHA, actual aberrations/extraplanar creatures, teleportation/incorporeality restrictions**, its missing charges field and exact private matrix metadata. **NEW operational use budget:** one lifetime activation, stored separately as `lifetimeActivations=1`, not a fabricated source charges value. Action with one hand within5ft on a properly placed complete RC-seal activates the stationary field; no concentration. One hour begins at activation. It cannot be moved to sweep targets: moving it ends the field and its activation remains spent. Suppression/cover/Broken prevents its field while that state applies; expiry runs and restoring state grants only any remaining lawful duration, never a fresh hour.

The ward affects only creatures within20ft and line of effect whose actual governing classification establishes one of the printed target labels. It does not classify people by appearance, ancestry, lore guesses or an unknown flag. It creates no new cosmology list. On an affected creature's **actual attempt to teleport or begin an incorporeal state/movement**, make one binary DC28 CHA save for that attempted effect. Success permits only that actual otherwise legal attempt; failure blocks it and leaves its already committed costs spent. No action/resource-free testing save, no recurring save merely for standing there, no immunity for the whole hour after one success. One save covers one whole actual teleport/incorporeal movement attempt, not a new save at every five-foot increment of that same movement. Failure prevents the restricted attempt, not unrelated ordinary corporeal movement. A continuing corporeal position is not forcibly altered; an already-incorporeal creature is not ejected into a wall. If it later tries a new incorporeal movement through the ward, that is a new actual restricted attempt. Involuntary teleportation of an eligible creature inside the field uses that creature's save; arrival by a source originating wholly outside the field is not retroactively prevented by checking a destination after arrival.

Its limits do not prevent ordinary walking, flight, forced movement or break an unrelated spell/portal. An actual source that ignores such a ward retains its own precedence; an unprovided target/teleport/incorporeal profile cannot be invented. This completes the ordinary ward algorithm while leaving classification and those actual effect records as bounded execution dependencies. Matrical-adjacent/Matrix references remain preserved, not interpreted or activated as research.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs.
